---
title: Full changelog for Home Assistant 2026.11
description: Detailed changelog for the Home Assistant 2026.11 release
---

These are all the changes included in the Home Assistant 2026.11 release.
For a summary in a more readable format
[Release notes blog for this release](/blog/2026/11/04/release-202611/).

## All changes

- [Backend](#backend)
- [Frontend](#frontend)

### Backend

- Add reconfigure flow for Anthropic ([@Shulyaka] - [#183793])
- Bump version to 2026.11.0dev0 ([@frenck] - [#183795])
- Implement a classlevel ReadOnlyDict instead of a dict per instance for allowed_context_keys in Selector classes ([@jbouwh] - [#183761])
- Mark CONF_TOKEN as secret in credential schemas ([@frenck] - [#183796])
- Adjust mqtt to not override Entity.add_to_platform_xxx methods ([@emontnemery] - [#183776])
- Bump besen to 0.4.8 ([@moryoav] - [#183800])

- Merge duplicate UpdateFailed raises in the Sofar coordinator ([@darkrain-nl] - [#183797])

- Update dependency typescript-eslint to v8.72.0 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55199])
- Add query params to the DHCP, SSDP and Zeroconf tables ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55195])
- Update dependency @codemirror/view to v6.43.15 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55201])
- Add two way audio support (frontend) ([@starkillerOG] - [frontend#54660])
- Update dependency intl-messageformat to v12.1.4 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55207])
- Update formatjs (@renovatebot - [frontend#55208])
- Update Node.js to v24.22.1 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55210])
- Show device instead of unknown entity for device actions in trace ([@pradeeppeddineni] - [frontend#55200])
- Add query params to the ZHA groups table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55194])
- Add query params to the application credentials table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55143])
- Add translation_key filter to entity selectors ([@DaanVervacke] - [frontend#55102])
- Energy usage graph: don't combine grid sources over a negligible value ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55214])
- Translate show more items in trace timeline ([@pradeeppeddineni] - [frontend#55206])
- Add query params to the statistics table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55219])
- Drop stale related lookups in the data table filters ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55218])
- Add query params to the Z-Wave provisioned devices table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55216])
- Add query params to the infrared and radio frequency devices tables ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55217])
- Show unattributed grid export in energy distribution card ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55213])
- Add query params to the automations table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55171])
- Update ESPHome setup wizard audio and serial copy ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55212])
- Apply UX feedback to target picker exclusions ([@jpbede] - [frontend#55198])
- Find ESPHome setup switches through the device wizard ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55211])
- Update dependency xss to v1.0.16 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55226])
- Refresh the target picker when its filters change ([@piitaya] - [frontend#55186])
- Open the add integration dialog for brand links ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55227])
- Add query params to the entities table ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55105])
- Remove "Perform action" prefix from automation action rows ([@marcinbauer-ohf] - [frontend#55228])
- Show section backgrounds in the sections view sidebar ([@piitaya] - [frontend#55234])
- Add a preview and a clearer width setting to the section editor ([@piitaya] - [frontend#54961])
- Allow picking several categories in the category filter ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55222])
- Use the Sendspin mark and check badges in the ESPHome setup wizard ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55239])
- Show bridged devices on the Matter network map ([@mib1185] - [frontend#54812])
- Keep the integrations dashboard and integration page search in the URL ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55225])
- Use the offset duration mode for sun and calendar offsets ([@piitaya] - [frontend#54703])
- Call the Z-Wave adapter a device in the ESPHome setup wizard ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55196])
- Update device class constants (@github-actions - [frontend#55250])
- Update dependency vite to v8.3.5 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55253])
- Enlarge the Sendspin mark in the ESPHome setup wizard ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55252])
- Update pnpm to v12.15.0 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55261])
- Update dependency @lezer/common to v1.5.4 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55265])
- Update release-drafter/release-drafter action to v7.10.0 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55280])
- Update github/codeql-action action to v4.39.0 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55279])
- Update dependency @types/chromecast-caf-receiver to v7 (@renovatebot - [frontend#55274])
- Add AI Task evaluate types and method ([@balloob] - [frontend#55281])
- Fix Enter on Android moving focus away from todo list add item input ([@pradeeppeddineni] - [frontend#55256])
- Add action history page to tools ([@joostlek] - [frontend#55078])
- Scroll the Assist chat to the newest message ([@cpruijsen] - [frontend#55240])
- Revert "Add translation_key filter to entity selectors" ([@timmo001] - [frontend#55282])
- Add Paste to the dashboard card menu ([@maartenla] - [frontend#55215])
- Add copy as YAML button to action history detail dialog ([@joostlek] - [frontend#55284])
- Add Date card ([@pcan08] - [frontend#52994])
- Share one log viewer between the live log dialog and the Z-Wave logs page ([@maartenla] - [frontend#55057])
- Fix cast receiver types for chromecast-caf-receiver 7 ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55288])
- Show progress and result when checking for app updates ([@craig-b] - [frontend#55205])
- Add ripple and haptic feedback to control number buttons ([@dschace] - [frontend#55156])
- Fix extra unit-less statistics chart in history graphs ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#55268])
